## 24. Ujuzi Sandbox (v3.8.0 +)

Vikomo na vifungo vya usalama hutumika wakati mfumo wa Ujuzi (`src/lib/skills/`) unatekeleza otomatiki zilizobainishwa na mtumiaji katika mazingira ya sanduku la mchanga.

| Inaweza kubadilika                | Chaguomsingi                                | Faili Chanzo                                                       | Maelezo                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| --------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (sek.10)                            | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Muda wa kila utekelezaji wa saa ya ukutani umekwisha kwa msimbo wa ujuzi wa kisanduku cha mchanga. Kofia ngumu; chochote tena kinauawa.                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(inarudi kwa `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Muda wa ochestration wa hali ya juu umeisha. Weka juu zaidi ya `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` ili kuruhusu utiririshaji wa hatua nyingi.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                            | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ujuzi wa juu unaweza kusoma kutoka kwa faili yoyote ya kisanduku cha mchanga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                           | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Upeo wa baiti zilizonaswa kutoka kwa jibu lolote HTTP ndani ya ujuzi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                    | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Kofia ngumu kwenye herufi stdout/stderr imerejeshwa kutoka kwa ombi la kisanduku cha mchanga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                     | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Weka `1`/`true` ili kuruhusu mtandao wa nje kutoka ndani ya sandbox. Chaguomsingi kwa** kutengwa** kwa usalama.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(tupu)_                                    | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Orodha ya ruhusa iliyotenganishwa kwa koma ya picha za kontena zinazoruhusiwa kwa utekelezaji wa kisanduku cha mchanga. Tupu inamaanisha chaguomsingi iliyojumuishwa pekee.                                                                                                                                             |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(chaguo-msingi iliyojengwa ndani)_         | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Picha ya chombo kilichotumika wakati wa kutengeneza kisanduku cha mchanga kinachoungwa mkono na Docker. Batilisha ili kubandika picha maalum ya msingi mgumu.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                      | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Muda wa matumizi ya chombo kwa ustadi wa kutengeneza sandbox: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` huchagua wakati bora zaidi wa kusakinisha kwa kila seva pangishi OS (Apple Container/OrbStack kwenye macOS, WSL Container kwenye Windows, Podman kwenye Linux), ikirejea Docker. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Kuwezesha `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` hufungua njia ya kutoka kutoka kwa msimbo wa ujuzi wa kiholela. Oanisha na `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` na sera madhubuti ya `CORS_ORIGIN`/proksi katika usambazaji wa pamoja.

## 25. Nafasi za Watoa Huduma, Vichuguu, Hifadhi rudufu na Muda wa Ruhusa Zingine

Miisho ya mgao wa watoa huduma, vichuguu vya mtandao (Tailscale, Ngrok, proksi ya utatuzi MITM ), hifadhi ya 1Proksi ya egress, hifadhi rudufu za hifadhidata na ubatilishaji mdogo wa kila kipengele unaorejelewa na safu ya mtekelezaji au hati.

| Inaweza kubadilika                             | Chaguomsingi                                                                | Faili Chanzo                                                              | Maelezo                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_429`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Njia mbadala ya kila hali wezesha kwa hitilafu `429`. Wakati `false` (na `OMNIROUTE_ROTATION_ENABLED=true`), `429` haisababishi tena mzunguko wa akaunti na inarejeshwa kwa mteja badala yake.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Idadi ya makosa `429` ndani ya `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS` inahitajika kabla ya akaunti kuzungushwa. `1` (chaguo-msingi) huzunguka mara moja, kuhifadhi tabia ya kihistoria.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Dirisha la kuteleza (sekunde) ambalo makosa `429` yanahesabiwa kuelekea `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_429_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_500`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Urudishaji wa hali ya kila kuwezesha hitilafu za seva `5xx` (bila kujumuisha `502`, ambayo ina darasa lake). Wakati `false`, hitilafu hizi hazisababishi tena mzunguko wa akaunti.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Idadi ya makosa `5xx` ndani ya `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS` inahitajika kabla ya akaunti kuzungushwa. `1` (chaguo-msingi) huzunguka mara moja.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Dirisha la kuteleza (sekunde) ambalo makosa `5xx` yanahesabiwa kuelekea `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_500_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_502`                      | `true`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Njia mbadala ya kila hali huwezesha makosa ya `502` (lango mbaya). Wakati `false`, `502` s haisababishi tena mzunguko wa akaunti.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Idadi ya makosa `502` ndani ya `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS` inahitajika kabla ya akaunti kuzungushwa. `1` (chaguo-msingi) huzunguka mara moja.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Dirisha la kuteleza (sekunde) ambalo makosa `502` yanahesabiwa kuelekea `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400`                      | `false`                                                                     | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Jijumuishe (chaguo-msingi OFF): wakati `true`, `400` wazi (ombi baya) pia huanzisha mzunguko wa akaunti. Hii ni nyongeza pekee - haizuii kamwe tabia iliyopo ya injini ambapo maandishi ya `400` yenye rate-limit/quota bado yanaanguka bila kujali bendera hii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Idadi ya makosa `400` ndani ya `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS` inayohitajika kabla ya akaunti kuzungushwa (inashauriwa tu wakati `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Dirisha la kuteleza (sekunde) ambalo makosa `400` yanahesabiwa kuelekea `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

### Claude Mratibu wa Joto

Joto linaloendeshwa na Cron kwa miunganisho iliyojumuishwa ya Anthropic OAuth, kwa hivyo dirisha la kikomo cha viwango vya saa 5 hufunguliwa kwa ombi dogo lililoratibiwa badala ya la kwanza halisi (#8848). Kipanga ratiba kimezimwa isipokuwa `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` ni ya ukweli** na** muunganisho umealamishwa katika `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; orodha tupu ya unganisho inamaanisha kuwa hakuna chochote kinachowashwa moto hata na env var.

| Inaweza kubadilika             | Chaguomsingi                | Faili Chanzo                 | Maelezo                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(ondoa → )_                | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Swichi kuu ya kipanga ratiba cha joto. Inakubali `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (haijalishi, iliyopunguzwa). Thamani nyingine yoyote, au haijawekwa, humwacha kiratibu mbali.   |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Usemi wa cron wa sehemu-tano kwa tiki ya uboreshaji, iliyotathminiwa katika `America/Los_Angeles` ( saa za eneo la kuweka upya Anthropic) bila kujali saa ya mwenyeji. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Ni miunganisho mingapi inayopashwa joto sambamba kwa kila tiki. Imefungwa kwa `1`-`10`; thamani isiyo ya nambari inarudi kwa `3`.                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Mfano unaotumika kwa ombi la joto. Batilisha tu ikiwa chaguo-msingi haipatikani kwenye mpango wako; chagua mfano wa bei rahisi zaidi ambao bado unafungua dirisha.     |

### Ingia katika Kivinjari VNC Vipindi & Lakabu ya Data-Dir

Chromium +VNC iliyo katika kontena inayotumika kwa kunasa kitambulisho shirikishi cha kuingia kwenye kivinjari (`/api/vnc-session`), pamoja na lakabu `DATA_DIR` iliyopitwa na wakati. Yote ni ya hiari - chaguo-msingi za VNC zinalenga picha `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` iliyounganishwa na hubatilishwa tu kwa picha maalum ya kontena, milango au urekebishaji wa mzunguko wa maisha.

| Inaweza kubadilika                    | Chaguomsingi                                                             | Faili Chanzo                                | Maelezo                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Docker tagi ya chombo cha kuingia cha Chromium +VNC. Jenga `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` au uelekeze hii kwa picha maalum.                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Njia ya jozi ya wakati wa kukimbia ya kontena iliyotumiwa kuzindua kontena VNC (km kuweka `podman`).                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | VNC/noVNC bandari wazi ndani ya kontena.                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Mlango wa Itifaki ya Chrome DevTools ndani ya kontena.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Njia ya saraka ya wasifu kwenye Chromium ndani ya chombo.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Saraka ya seva pangishi inayoshikilia wasifu unaoendelea wa kuingia katika kivinjari.                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (dakika 10)                                                     | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Muda wa kutofanya kitu (ms) kabla ya kipindi kisichotumika VNC kuvunwa.                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (dakika 30)                                                    | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Kofia ngumu (ms) kwenye maisha ya kipindi VNC.                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Idadi ya juu zaidi ya vipindi vya VNC vinavyofanana.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Muda umeisha (ms) kusubiri kivinjari kilicho na vyombo kuwa tayari kwa CDP.                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Muda umeisha (ms) wa kuvuna session/cookies iliyonaswa baada ya kuingia kukamilika.                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Alama za ziada za mstari wa amri hupitishwa kwenye Chromium iliyo na chombo.                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Mtandao maalum wa Docker chombo cha kuingia VNC hujiunga (#12571) badala ya daraja chaguo-msingi, kwa hivyo makontena yanayohusiana hayawezi kufikia lango lake la daraja la CDP.                          |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(haijawekwa)_                                                           | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Lakabu za urithi** kwa `DATA_DIR`, imeangaliwa tu baada ya `DATA_DIR` na `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR` kutowekwa. Hutafuta akiba ya kipindi cha nyuzi za wavuti Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Mtihani & E2E Harness

Inatumiwa na `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`, `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, na `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`. Wacha kila thamani iliyo hapa chini ikiwa haijawekwa katika uwekaji wa matoleo ya umma.

| Inaweza kubadilika                     | Chaguomsingi                   | Faili Chanzo                              | Maelezo                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| -------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | E2E bootstrap mode (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) kwa kikimbiaji Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | inarudi kwa `INITIAL_PASSWORD` | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Nenosiri la msimamizi limeingizwa kwenye mazingira Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Zima kura ya ukaguzi wa afya ya eneo lako wakati wa utekelezaji wa Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Zima kitanzi cha OAuth token healthcheck wakati wa majaribio.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(haijawekwa)_                 | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Watoa huduma waliotenganishwa na koma hawajajumuishwa kwenye ufagiaji wa kuonyesha upya tokeni (km `codex,openai`). Njia mbadala inayolengwa ya kuzima kabisa watoa huduma wa — short-TTL wa cheki ya afya huendelea kuburudishwa huku watoa huduma wa mteremko wakiendelea kufanya kazi pekee. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                         | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Nyamazisha kelele za ukaguzi wa afya katika Playwright stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Ruka muundo wa uzalishaji wa Next.js kabla ya Playwright kuanza ( uboreshaji wa CI ).                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                            | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Ruka ndoano ya kufuta OmniRoute (inayotumiwa na CI kuweka `node_modules` sawa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                       | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Muda wa kusubiri (ms) ili seva iwe na afya njema kabla ya kufanya majaribio ecosystem/protocol.                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login` | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL chombo cha moshi Electron kinatarajia programu iliyopakiwa kutumika.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                        | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Jumla ya muda umekwisha (ms) kabla kifaa cha moshi kukata tamaa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                         | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Tatua dirisha (ms) baada ya upakiaji wa ukurasa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(oto)_                        | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Njia ya wazi ya Electron iliyofungwa inayoweza kutekelezwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                     | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Saraka ya data ya Electron moshi kukimbia.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Weka `1` kuhifadhi saraka ya data ya moshi baada ya kukimbia.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Weka `1` kutiririsha kumbukumbu za Electron ili stdout wakati wa kukimbia.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                            | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: zindua upya dhidi ya dir sawa ya data na udai kuwa uzinduzi wa pili utachagua kiendesha SQLite asili.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(kuangalia PATH )_            | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Batilisha njia ya binary ya Devin CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

### Bomba la kutafsiri hati

Inatumiwa na `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` (amri ya `npm run i18n:run` ). Vigezo vyote sita havijawekwa kwa chaguo-msingi — huviweka katika `.env` pekee kwenye mashine ambazo zinafaa kuwa na uwezo wa kuendesha kitafsiri cha hati.

| Inaweza kubadilika                         | Chaguomsingi   | Faili Chanzo                              | Maelezo                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------ | -------------- | ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`            | _(haijawekwa)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Msingi wa URL unaolingana na OpenAI kwa mandharinyuma ya tafsiri.                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`            | _(haijawekwa)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Tokeni ya mbebaji kwa mazingira ya nyuma ya tafsiri (haijaingia kamwe).                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`              | _(haijawekwa)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Kitambulisho cha mfano, kwa mfano `gpt-4o-mini` au `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_FALLBACK_MODELS`    | _(haijawekwa)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs`  | Msururu wa kielelezo uliotenganishwa kwa hiari na koma ulijaribiwa kwa mpangilio wakati muundo msingi umeshindwa (mgawo wa 429, 401/402, 5xx, muda umeisha, mtandao); ombi mbovu (400/413/422) halijaribiwi tena mahali pengine. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`         | `60000`        | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Muda wa ombi umekwisha katika milisekunde.                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_REASONING_EFFORT`   | _(haijawekwa)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs`  | Udhibiti wa hiari wa hoja, unaotumwa kama-ulivyo kwa mazingira ya nyuma wakati umewekwa.                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY`        | `4`            | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Maombi ya tafsiri sambamba wakati wa kutumia faili / lugha nyingi.                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_ENGINE`             | `llm`          | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | `llm` hutumia backend hapo juu; `google` hutafsiri kupitia injini Google iliyo salama ya muundo na haihitaji vigeuzo vyovyote vya LLM.                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_FALLBACK`    | `1`            | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`        | Na injini ya `llm`, chunk ambayo mtindo haukuweza kutoa (mlinzi wa pato alishindwa kwa kila jaribio, au kosa la mfano) hutafsiriwa na Google badala ya kushindwa eneo. `0` inazima.                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_API_KEY`     | _(haijawekwa)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Kitufe cha Hiari cha Tafsiri ya Wingu v2. Isiyowekwa hutumia sehemu ya mwisho ya wavuti isiyo na ufunguo (kikomo cha bei, faini kwa mawimbi ya hapa na pale).                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_CONCURRENCY` | `6`            | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Maombi ya Google sambamba yanayoshirikiwa na kila eneo la uendeshaji.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_GOOGLE_TIMEOUT_MS`  | `30000`        | `scripts/i18n/lib/google-translate.mjs`   | Muda wa ombi umekwisha kwa injini Google, kwa milisekunde.                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `UI_KIT_READ_TOKEN`                        | _(haijawekwa)_ | `scripts/check/check-ui-kit-upstream.mjs` | Tokeni ya kusoma tu ya repo ya faragha UI kit repo, inayotumiwa na `npm run check:ui-kit-upstream` na mtiririko wa kazi wa kila wiki wa "UI Kit upstream watch". Ndani ya nchi cheki hutumia vitambulisho vyako vya git.         |

## 27. Mlisho wa Rada (Kujihudumia)

Programu jalizi ya hiari iliyowekwa na bendera ya kipengele cha RADAR_ENABLED (chaguo-msingi imezimwa - kipengele cha bendera kilichobadilishwa kupitia Settings/DB, si env var; angalia [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off) ). Vigezo vinne vya kwanza hapa chini ni ubatilishaji wa hiari wa mlisho unaojipangisha au uliogawanyika na mitiririko ya ufunguo wa tegemezi. Ya tano, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`, ni kiungo tofauti kisicho na chaguo-msingi kwa paneli ya uendeshaji ya kibinafsi ya mmiliki. Tazama [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) kwa hati kamili ya moduli na [uanzishaji wake kutoka mwisho hadi mwisho na mlolongo wa usanidi wa mwongozo](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

Kisomaji cha tangazo la jumla Home/Changelog hakijasanidiwa na tofauti ya mazingira na haitegemei alama ya kipengele cha RADAR_ENABLED. Inasoma hazina ya umma `news.json` URL iliyotangazwa katika `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts` na GET pekee; kufukuzwa IDs kubaki katika hifadhi ya ndani ya kivinjari.

| Inaweza kubadilika            | Chaguomsingi                                 | Faili Chanzo                                                 | Maelezo                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | URL msingi hushirikiwa na katalogi iliyotiwa saini tofauti, marejeleo, matoleo ya wafadhili na milisho ya Intel. Batilisha ili uelekeze kwenye huduma inayojiendesha yenyewe au iliyogawanyika.     |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(ufunguo chaguomsingi umebandikwa)_         | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Ufunguo wa umma wa Ed25519 (base64-DER SPKI au PEM) hutumika kuthibitisha saini za mipasho kutoka kwa mipasho maalum.                                                                               |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL kitufe cha dashibodi cha "Mimi ni mchangiaji" hufunguka ( mtiririko wa dai la ufunguo wa GitHub OAuth ).                                                                                        |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL kitufe cha dashibodi cha "Saidia mradi" hufungua ( ukurasa wa payment/plans ).                                                                                                                  |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(haijawekwa)_                               | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Kiungo cha paneli ya shughuli za kibinafsi za mmiliki pekee. HTTPS inahitajika isipokuwa kwa kitanzi cha nyuma SSH HTTP mbele; thamani ambazo hazijawekwa au batili haziunda kipengee cha kusogeza. |

## Ukaguzi: Vigezo Vilivyoondolewa / Vilivyokufa

Vigezo vifuatavyo vilionekana katika matoleo ya awali ya `.env.example` lakini yana** hakuna marejeleo ya wakati wa utekelezaji** katika codebase ya sasa. Wameondolewa:

| Inaweza kubadilika                                                                                                                                                              | Sababu                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Kamwe usisome na faili yoyote ya chanzo. SQLite ndiye kiendeshi pekee kinachotumika - hakuna uteuzi unaohitajika.                                                       |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Wasilisha violezo vya zamani docs/env lakini haitumiki wakati wa utekelezaji. Inaweza kurudi katika kipengele cha baadaye cha matukio mengi.                            |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | Haijarejelewa katika msimbo wa chanzo. Ukubwa wa hifadhidata sio mdogo kwa njia bandia.                                                                                 |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | Haijarejelewa katika msimbo wa chanzo. Usafishaji wa urithi uliwezekana kuondolewa.                                                                                     |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | Haijasajiliwa katika `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`.                                                                                                               |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | Haijasajiliwa katika `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` (Kimi Coding hutumia OAuth, sio CLI binary).                                                                   |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | Haijarejelewa popote katika msimbo wa chanzo.                                                                                                                           |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | Imeondolewa katika v3.8.0. Muda wa utekelezaji hausomi tena vazi hizi za env — vitambulisho vinatoka kwa Dashibodi / `data/provider-credentials.json` / iliyosimbwa DB. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | Imeondolewa katika v3.8.0. mtekelezaji Cursor hutumia `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (ona §22).                                                                 |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | Imeondolewa katika v3.8.0. Kiro iko katika `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` - kugeuza kwake hakuna athari.                                                             |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | Imeondolewa pamoja na vibao vingine vya API vya mtoa huduma ambavyo havijatumika katika v3.8.0.                                                                         |

### Marekebisho ya Thamani Chaguomsingi

| Inaweza kubadilika        | Thamani ya zamani `.env.example` | Chaguomsingi ya Msimbo Halisi | Imerekebishwa                                                          |
| ------------------------- | -------------------------------- | ----------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                             | `7`                           | ✅ Imeondolewa thamani inayopotosha; kumbukumbu `7` kama chaguo-msingi |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                             | `7`                           | ✅ Imeondolewa thamani inayopotosha; kumbukumbu `7` kama chaguo-msingi |

### uundaji upya wa usanidi OpenCode (zana za ad-hoc)

Inatumiwa na `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` kutengeneza tena `opencode.json` yenye thamani sahihi za `limit.context` na `limit.output` kutoka kwa mfano wa OmniRoute unaoendelea. Hakuna kati ya hizi zinazohitajika kwa operesheni ya kawaida - hati ni zana ya msanidi pekee.

| Inaweza kubadilika | Chaguomsingi             | Faili Chanzo                              | Maelezo                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Msingi wa URL wa mfano wa OmniRoute kuuliza `/v1/models`.                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(haijawekwa)_           | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | API ufunguo wa kuthibitisha dhidi ya mwisho wa OmniRoute `/v1/models`. Hurudi hadi `OPENCODE_API_KEY` wakati haijawekwa.                         |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(haijawekwa)_           | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Kitufe cha API mtindo wa OpenCode (`sk-...`) kimeandikwa kwenye `opencode.json` iliyofanywa upya. Hurudi hadi `OMNIROUTE_KEY` wakati haijawekwa. |

### Mfinyazo nje ya mtandao-eval harness (zana za ad-hoc)

Inatumiwa na `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts`, tathmini ya mbano nje ya mtandao CLI. Haihitajiki kwa operesheni ya kawaida - zana za msanidi pekee.

| Inaweza kubadilika           | Chaguomsingi | Faili Chanzo                        | Maelezo                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ---------------------------- | ------------ | ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (tupu)  | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Vitambulisho vya JSON vinavyotolewa na opereta kwa mtoa huduma vinavyotekelezwa na mbano-eval CLI ya nje ya mtandao (ikichanganuliwa na `JSON.parse`). Acha bila kuweka kwa kukimbia kavu. |

### VNC Vikao vya Kivinjari

Inatumiwa na `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` kusanidi vipindi vya Chromium visivyo na kichwa kulingana na Docker kwa watoa huduma wa otomatiki wa kivinjari. Zote za hiari - chaguo-msingi zimeonyeshwa hapa chini.

| Inaweza kubadilika                    | Chaguomsingi                   | Faili Chanzo                                | Maelezo                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Njia ya Docker binary inayotumiwa kuibua vyombo vya VNC.                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Docker picha ya chombo cha VNC Chromium.                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(bendera zilizojengwa ndani)_ | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Args za ziada za Chromium CLI hupitishwa kwa kivinjari ndani ya kontena.                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | VNC bandari ndani ya kontena.                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Mlango wa Itifaki ya Chrome DevTools ndani ya kontena.                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Saraka ya wasifu ndani ya kontena.                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(haijawekwa)_                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Saraka ya upande wa mwenyeji kwa wasifu unaoendelea wa kivinjari.                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Muda wa kutofanya kitu (ms) kabla ya kipindi cha VNC kuvunwa.                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Muda wa juu zaidi wa kipindi (ms).                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Upeo wa vipindi VNC vinavyofanana.                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Muda wa utayari wa kivinjari umekwisha (ms).                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Harvest/cleanup muda umeisha (ms).                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Mtandao wa Docker uliojitolea ambao kontena hujiunga (#12571), nje ya daraja chaguo-msingi. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(haijawekwa)_                 | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Saraka ya uendelevu wa kipindi cha Notion.                                                  |

### Hati ya huduma ya ndani

| Inaweza kubadilika                      | Chaguomsingi | Maelezo                                                                                                         |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`      | –            | Tokeni ya mstari wa uthibitishaji wa huduma kwa huduma ya usimamizi-ndege.                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE` | –            | Njia ya faili iliyo na tokeni ya huduma ya ndani (inayopendelewa katika vyombo; hubatilisha utofauti wa ndani). |

### takwimu za mtoa huduma OpenRouter

| Inaweza kubadilika                  | Chaguomsingi | Maelezo                                                                                             |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED` | `true`       | Weka kuwa `false` ili kuruka takwimu za OpenRouter kwa kila mtoa huduma kwa uboreshaji wa katalogi. |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`  | `3600000`    | Akiba TTL (ms) kwa takwimu za mtoaji wa OpenRouter zilizoletwa.                                     |

### Kufunga Redis iliyopachikwa

| Inaweza kubadilika          | Chaguomsingi | Maelezo                                                              |
| --------------------------- | ------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`           | `127.0.0.1`  | Unganisha anwani ya huduma iliyopachikwa Redis.                      |
| `REDIS_PORT`                | `6379`       | Bandari kwa huduma iliyopachikwa Redis.                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST` | –            | Kubatilisha kwa upeo wa OmniRoute kwa anwani ya Redis iliyopachikwa. |

### ChatGPT Mtandao (Codex)

Chaguomsingi za Globale kwa kutumia Kivinjari kisicho na kichwa na kugeuza Zana-Handaki. Im Dashibodi igeuze Connection-Werte haben Vorrang.

| Inaweza kubadilika                      | Chaguomsingi                   | Faili Chanzo                                                               | Maelezo                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _(gundua kiotomatiki)_         | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Expliziter Chrome-/Chromium-Pfad für npm-, systemd- und PM2-Betrieb.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _(gundua kiotomatiki)_         | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Gemeinsamer Fallback Chrome-/Chromium-Pfad. zaidi                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(haijawekwa)_                 | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Ndani CDP-Endpunkt; Docker verwendet den Sidecar auf Port `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_USE_CDP_PROFILE`     | _(ondoa — )_                   | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/persistent-cdp-context.ts`              | Weka kwa `1` (na sehemu ya mwisho ya CDP iliyosanidiwa) ili kutumia tena ukurasa wa ChatGPT unaodhibitiwa na nje, ulioingia katika wasifu wa kivinjari badala ya muktadha uliojitenga kwa kila zamu; pia hufunika tabo za ChatGPT zinazofanana kwa 1. Zima kwa chaguo-msingi. Tazama [wasifu wa CDP unaoendelea](../guides/CHATGPT-WEB-PERSISTENT-CDP.md). |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(haijawekwa)_                 | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                           | Wenn gesetzt, muss jede Anfrage an CDP-Proxy-Sidecar diesen Wert im Header `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` mitschicken (#13679). Ohne Wert leitet der Proxy unauthentifiziert weiter — dann schützt nur die Netzisolierung des Compose-Netzes `chatgpt-web-codex-net`. Erzeugen mit `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                             |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(haijawekwa)_                 | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Globale OpenAI-Tunnel-ID kutoka kwa Codex-Tool-Runden ya lokale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(haijawekwa)_                 | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Globaler Tunnel Runtime-Key; niemas katika Kumbukumbu ausgeben.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Jina la Exakter des neu erstellten ChatGPT-Custom-Connectors kwa ajili ya MCP-Brücke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Dediziertes Verzeichnis für Browser-, Broker- und Tunnelzustand.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-worker.ts` | Angalia `1` Browser-Diagnosebilder na Checkpoint moja kwa moja.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(haijawekwa)_                 | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Optionaler absoluter Pfad zu einem dauerhaften Launcher-Binary.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _(gundua kiotomatiki)_         | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Optionaler absoluter Pfad zum Bun-Runtime-Binary.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(haijawekwa)_                 | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Legacy-Fallback für `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; neue Mipangilio verwenden den kanonischen Name.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

---

## OmniConductor Bridge

Mtumiaji wa SSE wa muda mrefu anayeakisi kazi za kitovu cha OmniConductor katika A2A TaskManager ya ndani (`src/lib/conductor/`). Jijumuishe - daraja huanza tu wakati `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL` imewekwa. Upande wa seva pekee: tokeni ya kitovu haipaswi kamwe kufikia kivinjari.

| Inaweza kubadilika             | Chaguomsingi            | Faili Chanzo                     | Maelezo                                                                                                                    |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(tupu)_                | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Msingi URL wa kitovu cha OmniConductor (km `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). Daraja la = halijawekwa limezimwa.                    |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(tupu)_                | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Kitambulisho cha kitovu cha mlisho wa SSE - toa rika `spokesperson` -kind kwenye kitovu (`POST /v1/peers`, admin).         |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(tupu)_                | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | Kitambulisho cha ujumbe wa kazi ya kitovu cha A2A→(`POST /v1/tasks`); inarudi kwa `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN` wakati haijawekwa. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | URL ya msingi ya huduma ya msemaji (Faro) nyuma ya seva mbadala ya gumzo ya dashibodi (`/api/conductor/ask`).              |

### Ratiba inayofahamu kiasi

Inatumiwa na `open-sse/services/combo.ts` na `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` kwa ukaguzi wa bajeti ya ombi la mapema. Jijumuishe - tabia ya uelekezaji chaguo-msingi haibadilishwa ikiwa haijawekwa.

| Inaweza kubadilika                 | Chaguomsingi  | Faili Chanzo                                | Maelezo                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| ---------------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING`    | `0`           | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                | Wakati `1`, ruka miunganisho ambayo bajeti ya tokeni kwa kila dirisha (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, jedwali `provider_quota_state`) haiwezi kumudu makadirio ya gharama ya ombi kabla ya kutumwa. Imeshindwa kufungua wakati hakuna bajeti iliyosanidiwa.                |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`  | _(imewashwa)_ | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts` | Urekebishaji wa maoni ya mkadiriaji wa tokeni ya mlinzi wa muktadha (#14931): kwa (mtoa huduma, modeli, zana zilizopo) EMA ya `usage.prompt_tokens` / kadirio, iliyobanwa hadi 0.15–2.0, katika kumbukumbu pekee. `off`, `0` au `false` hulemaza kurekodi na utumaji. |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED` | `false`       | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`          | Husajili njia za picha `xai-oauth` (`xao`) na `grok-cli`. Bendera ya kipengele cha dashibodi ya jina moja; ubatilishaji wa DB unashinda thamani hii.                                                                                                                  |
